slice/book-writing-project/00 · detail

Intent: A detail view for one chapter in my book writing project. I need a screen where I can see all the info for a single chapter at a glance:

* show the "Chapter Title" as "2. The Beacon Light"
* show the "Manuscript" it's part of as "The Lighthouse Keeper's Widow"
* show the "Word Count" as "2750"
* show the "POV Character" as "Eliza Gray"
* show the "Status" as "Not Started"
* show the "Completion" as "5%"
* have a button that says "Move to Next Manuscript" to advance it

k=0 PASS · 339 tok
<a2ui>
surface("gallery-detail-view", sendDataModel=true)
$/chapterTitle = "2. The Beacon Light"
$/manuscript = "The Lighthouse Keeper's Widow"
$/wordCount = 2750
$/povCharacter = "Eliza Gray"
$/status = "Not Started"
$/completion = 5
root = Card(main_column)
main_column = Column([chapter_title, info_col, action_btn])
chapter_title = Text($/chapterTitle, "h1")
info_col = Column([manuscript_row, word_count_row, pov_row, status_row, completion_row])
manuscript_row = Row([manuscript_label, manuscript_value], "spaceBetween")
manuscript_label = Text("Manuscript")
manuscript_value = Text($/manuscript)
word_count_row = Row([word_count_label, word_count_value], "spaceBetween")
word_count_label = Text("Word Count")
word_count_value = Text(formatNumber($/wordCount))
pov_row = Row([pov_label, pov_value], "spaceBetween")
pov_label = Text("POV Character")
pov_value = Text($/povCharacter)
status_row = Row([status_label, status_value], "spaceBetween")
status_label = Text("Status")
status_value = Text($/status)
completion_row = Row([completion_label, completion_value], "spaceBetween")
completion_label = Text("Completion")
completion_value = Text(formatString("${/completion}%"))
action_btn = Button(move_btn_text, _, Event("move_to_next_manuscript", {}))
move_btn_text = Text("Move to Next Manuscript")
</a2ui>

The Unsent Letter" with "3100", "First Draft", "No"; and "5. The Council of Thorns" with "5120", "Needs Rewrite", "No"
* I need a way to narrow it down by "Status" with the choices "Not Started", "First Draft", "Revised", "Needs Rewrite", and "Final"
* And please put a search box at the top so I can quickly find a chapter
